# Full-row package benchmark comparison

Timing units are nanoseconds. p50 is the median of block medians; p95 is the sorted retained sample at index floor(0.95 × (N − 1)). All retained samples are included. Status, p, and 95% CI come from the preserved Labs comparison output. Labs classifies block medians with exact Mann–Whitney U (alpha 0.05) and an absolute Hodges–Lehmann delta of at least 5%. Reported p50 delta is a separate median ratio. This classifier is not a user admission threshold; neutral does not establish parity or zero effect.

First candidate raw JSON: `/Volumes/Depot/Developer/glyph/.cache/held-wave2-20261020/evidence/matrix-main-data/candidate.json` (SHA-256 `07379e474976e9b84a2a552989031f09bdd8645ae0a0d2940f8bed396bcfb8b7`). Each completed arm must carry byte-identical candidate JSON.
